# Agents · I · part 2

- **impl-judge**: LLM-as-Judge for implementation completeness. Compares code changes against the plan, checks handler wiring, route connectivity, import completeness, environment dependencies, and side effect invocation. Use after implementing a feature to verify nothing was missed. ([dan1d/claude-ninja](https://github.com/dan1d/claude-ninja/blob/HEAD/agents/impl-judge.md))
- **impl-maintenance**: Post-session maintenance agent. Reads what happened during an implementation, fix, or upgrade session and produces a structured Lessons Learned report with actionable suggestions for improving project tooling — CLAUDE.md rules, reference docs, hooks, command workflows, and new skill patterns. Suggest-only; does NOT write files. ([ihudak/ihudak-claude-plugins](https://github.com/ihudak/ihudak-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/workflows-core/agents/impl-maintenance.md) · ★2)
